Includes data preparation scripts, config templates, and FastAPI serving examples. ([SpillwaveSolutions/spacy-nlp-agentic-skill](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/spacy-nlp-agentic-skill))
- **spade**: Scope, Plan, Approve, Deliver, Evaluate - 19 skills, eight reviewer personas, one researcher, and six helpers. ([source](.))
- **spades**: Scope, Plan, Approve, Deliver, Evaluate, Ship — six-phase loop with a Project layer and pluggable backends (Linear MCP, local filesystem, more). 23 skills and 5 agents (4 reviewer personas + 1 researcher). Pure Markdown, no bash, no external setup. ([ChrisFmlyc/spades](https://github.com/ChrisFmlyc/spades/tree/HEAD/plugins/spades))
- **spaitial-ai**: Generate and edit explorable 3D Gaussian-splat worlds from a text prompt, image, 360 panorama, or iterative pano edit using the SpAItial MCP server — then download splats, export meshes, manage your library, and preview them via hosted viewer links. ([spaitial-ai/spaitial-ai-plugin](https://github.com/spaitial-ai/spaitial-ai-plugin))
- **spamhole**: Claude Code plugin: AI-assisted defenses against pseudo-personalised wide-scrape outreach, AI-faked impersonation, and tracking-pixel surveillance. Capture spam to a personal corpus, analyse intent, suggest filter patterns, scan for tracking + ad-tracker pixels, draft unsubscribe replies, push server-side Gmail blocks via an email MCP, push DNS-level blocks to AdGuard Home, and contribute redacted findings to public anti-tracking lists. Bundles a stub AdGuard Home MCP. ([danielrosehill/Claude-SpamHole-Plugin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/Claude-SpamHole-Plugin))
- **spamtitan**: SpamTitan by TitanHQ - quarantine queue management, email flow stats, sender allowlist/blocklist for MSPs ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/spamtitan/spamtitan))
- **span**: Query engineering, project management, investment, and AI agent trace data from the Span Knowledge Graph API ([Attuned-Corp/skills](https://github.com/Attuned-Corp/skills))
- **spanglish**: Habla y escribe en spanglish de desarrollo: español para la narración, inglés para el código y los términos técnicos. ([naquiroz/spanglish](https://github.com/naquiroz/spanglish))
- **spanish-intake-skill**: Conducts bilingual (English/Spanish) client intake and communication: turns Spanish-language intake notes or a voicemail transcript into a structured English intake summary for the file, and turns an English draft communication into a natural Spanish version — flagging any ambiguous translation for the attorney's confirmation rather than guessing. ([protomated/protomated-plugins-official](https://github.com/protomated/protomated-plugins-official/tree/HEAD/spanish-intake-skill))
- **spanish-practice**: Práctica interactiva de español: ejercicios por tema y nivel (B1/B2), registro de lo aprendido y repaso espaciado. ([tjmgregory/.dotfiles](https://github.com/tjmgregory/.dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/plugins/spanish-practice))
- **spanish-research-grants**: Guía completa para preparar propuestas de investigación para convocatorias españolas (ISCIII, AEI). Cubre proyectos PI, PGC, becas y contratos del Sistema Nacional de Ciencia. ([pazulet/spanish-research-grants](https://github.com/pazulet/spanish-research-grants))
- **spanner** ([gemini-cli-extensions/spanner](https://github.com/gemini-cli-extensions/spanner))
- **spanner**: ~90 tok always-on. Google Cloud Spanner best practices — schema design, interleaving, indexes, transactions, query/DML optimization, schema evolution, and the Go client SDK. - **spanner-readonly-mcp**: Read-only Google Cloud Spanner MCP server. Exposes SELECT-only query execution and schema introspection; writes are blocked by a read-only snapshot. ([nu0ma/spanner-readonly-mcp](https://github.com/nu0ma/spanner-readonly-mcp))

- **spanreed**: Inter-agent message bus: register sessions, exchange messages, escalate to user when needed. ([Monkopedia/spanreed](https://github.com/Monkopedia/spanreed/tree/HEAD/plugins/spanreed))
- **spantail**: Capture Claude Code sessions as Spantail agent telemetry, log work entries, and build reports. ([spantail/spantail](https://github.com/spantail/spantail/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude-code))
- **spara**: Connect Claude to your Spara workspace to inspect and edit GTM agents as drafts, analyze your data, and search Spara documentation. ([spara-ai/spara-plugins](https://github.com/spara-ai/spara-plugins))

- **sparc-methodology**: SPARC agents and coordinator templates for systematic development methodology ([jmanhype/claude-code-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/jmanhype/claude-code-plugin-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/sparc-methodology))
- **sparda**: Build and interact with secure local MCP servers from Express and FastAPI backends. ([zakariagharzouli/sparda-skills](https://github.com/zakariagharzouli/sparda-skills))
- **sparda-gate**: The behavior gate for AI edits — proves each edit lost no auth guard, dropped no route, grew no blast radius. Deterministic, &lt;2s, offline, zero API key. ([zakariagharzouli/sparda](https://github.com/zakariagharzouli/sparda/tree/HEAD/integrations/claude-code-plugin))
